   518	/-- **Block coordinates** — the flat coords at layer `ℓ` CAPPED on the col axis by the running-min block
   519	width `widthMinUpto d ℓ` (family ruling 2026-07-22, item 1: the residual block is `widthMinUpto`-capped, not
   520	the whole `d_{ℓ+1}×d_ℓ` layer — matches `canonCenterOf`'s cap :725). NO cleared-shrink — that is
   521	`canonCenter`'s extra, keeping `supportAt` (full capped block) DISTINCT from `canonCenter` (capped AND
   522	cleared-shrunk). Bridge-free (Finset-definable from `d`+`ℓ`); `∅` for `ℓ ≥ N`. Feeds `supportAt`. -/
   523	noncomputable def blockCoords (d : Fin (N + 1) → ℕ) (ℓ : ℕ) : Finset (Fin (flatDim d)) :=
   524	  (Finset.univ.filter (fun q : tupIdx d =>
   525	      (q.1.1 : ℕ) = ℓ ∧ (q.2 : ℕ) < widthMinUpto d ℓ)).image (tupIdxEquiv d)
   526	
   527	/-- **The geometric descending support `supportAt S J`** (elder ruling pp.18–21, 2026-07-22 — the WINNER;
   528	the two-clock deeper∪shed shape is the DISCARD). The Deg1 support DESCENDS per clear: at `J = 0` it is the
   529	current capped block `blockCoords d S` (layer `S`, `widthMinUpto`-capped); once a pivot is cleared (`J ≥ 1`)
   530	it descends to `blockCoords d (S+1)` — the deeper block the `P`-cofactor `C'^{(S+1)} = Q⁻¹·C^{(S+1)}` reads;
   531	at the LAST layer `S = L` (`S + 1 = N`) the descent EXHAUSTS
   532	to the born-unit `∅` (no `C^{(S+1)}` to absorb into — terminal collapse, p.19/21).
   533	
   534	CONVENTION (elder's anti-off-by-one, transcribed): 0-indexed layers, `S ∈ {0..L}`, `L = N − 1` the last
   535	matrix-layer index; `S = L ⟺ S + 1 = N` is the born-unit case (0↔1 shift matches the page's 1-indexed `S = L`),
   536	NO substantive ±1 offset. `supportAt` is the geometric Deg1 SUPPORT and is DISTINCT from the LEDGER center
   537	`canonCenter S J` (the static layer-`S` carve = the blow-up locus) — different sets; the two-clock's
   538	conflation of the two is retired. -/
   539	noncomputable def supportAt (d : Fin (N + 1) → ℕ) (S J : ℕ) : Finset (Fin (flatDim d)) :=
   540	  if J = 0 then blockCoords d S
   541	  else if S + 1 < N then blockCoords d (S + 1)
   542	  else ∅
   543	
   544	/-- **The support LAYER of a state** (family ruling 2026-07-22, item 4 — the `fromLayer` threshold, DRY).
   545	Tracks the layer `supportAt` descends to: `s.layer` before any clear (`cleared = 0`), else `s.layer + 1`
   546	(the descended layer). Feeds `Deg1SupportedSlot`'s `fromLayer` (per-layer degree ≤ 1 from here up). -/
   547	def supportLayerOf {N : ℕ} (s : ConState N) : ℕ :=
   548	  if s.cleared = 0 then s.layer else s.layer + 1
   549	
   550	/-- **The foldState step invariant at a path node, PARAMETRIC in the center `C`** (elder re-statement +
   551	fix (2) (A)). Two conjuncts: (1) a divisibility witness `q` for the accumulated `StepInv`; (2) the residual
   552	is `Deg1SupportedSlot` on `C` — the ∃c support-decomposition PLUS per-layer total-degree ≤ 1 from
   553	`supportLayerOf p.conState` (`PerLayerDeg1From`, the parent-h tighten). Conjunct (2) is what the δ=1 strict
   554	transform needs; the per-layer grade self-propagates (kills c≡1 + the shear-frame + the degree-2-Schur
   555	`de·s1·s2` countermodels). Carried through the fold; the support `C` is `supportAt S J` (elder ruling
   556	2026-07-22 — the geometric DESCENDING window, see `supportAt`), computed from the node's state. -/
   557	def FoldStepInvAt {N : ℕ} (d : Fin (N + 1) → ℕ) (e : (Fin (flatDim d) → ℝ) ≃ₜ Tuple (k := ℝ) d)
   558	    (C : Finset (Fin (flatDim d))) (p : TreePath d) : Prop :=
   559	  (∃ q : Fin (d (Fin.last N) * d 0) → Fin (foldNR d p) → (Fin (flatDim d) → ℝ) → ℝ,
   560	    StepInv (coreGen d e) (foldG d e p) (foldB d e p) (foldResid d e p) q (foldRegion d e p)) ∧
   561	    -- elder ruling 2026-07-22: support `C = supportAt` (geometric, descending); D⁺ = its complement `Cᶜ`
   562	    (∀ j, Deg1SupportedSlot d (foldResid d e p) j C (supportLayerOf p.conState) (foldRegion d e p))
   563	
   564	/-- **The foldState step invariant** — `∃ C, FoldStepInvAt C p`. Shape → assumed delivery → stated
   565	obligation: this is the third and final form of the same content — degree-1 was never optional
   566	bookkeeping; it was the invariant the paper's inductive statement carried all along (the residual IS
   567	the block matrix). -/
   568	def FoldStepInv {N : ℕ} (d : Fin (N + 1) → ℕ) (e : (Fin (flatDim d) → ℝ) ≃ₜ Tuple (k := ℝ) d)
   569	    (p : TreePath d) : Prop :=
   570	  ∃ C : Finset (Fin (flatDim d)), FoldStepInvAt d e C p
   571	
   572	/-! ### `LastLayerInv` — the last-layer (S=L) mixed invariant (fix (2), elder-final)
   573	
   574	At the last layer (`S = L = N − 1`, 0-indexed per the elder's convention), the residual is being CLEARED slot by slot: an uncleared slot is still
   575	center-linear (`Deg1SupportedSlot`), a cleared slot is a unit (nonvanishing at `0`). `LastLayerInv` is the
   576	per-slot DISJUNCTION of these two, carried alongside the accumulated `StepInv` divisibility. It admits the
   577	S=L discriminator that the interior guard `+1 < N` excludes (the 6th, LAYER-terminal-range, severance
   578	axis) and feeds the re-parented `terminal_edge_stepInv`. -/
   579	
   580	/-- **The last-layer (S=L) mixed invariant.** The accumulated `StepInv` divisibility (D), AND a per-slot
   581	DISJUNCTION: each residual slot is either degree-1 supported (`Deg1SupportedSlot`, an UNcleared slot) OR a
   582	unit nonvanishing at `0` (a CLEARED slot). The two disjuncts are MUTUALLY EXCLUSIVE on any region `∋ 0`
   583	(the left ⟹ `resid 0 = 0` via the ∃c support-decomposition — `∑_{i∈S} cᵢ·uᵢ` at `u = 0` is `0`; unit ⟹
   584	`resid 0 ≠ 0`), so the fully-cleared `C = ∅` corner is the all-unit disjunct, no fence needed. `FoldStepInvAt`
   585	subsumes into this (all slots
   586	take the left disjunct — `foldStepInvAt_to_lastLayerInv`); the re-parented `terminal_edge_stepInv`
   587	consumes it. -/
   588	def LastLayerInv {N : ℕ} (d : Fin (N + 1) → ℕ) (e : (Fin (flatDim d) → ℝ) ≃ₜ Tuple (k := ℝ) d)
   589	    (C : Finset (Fin (flatDim d))) (p : TreePath d) : Prop :=
   590	  (∃ q : Fin (d (Fin.last N) * d 0) → Fin (foldNR d p) → (Fin (flatDim d) → ℝ) → ℝ,
   591	    StepInv (coreGen d e) (foldG d e p) (foldB d e p) (foldResid d e p) q (foldRegion d e p)) ∧
   592	    (∀ j : Fin (foldNR d p),
   593	      -- elder ruling 2026-07-22: support `C = supportAt` (geometric, descending); D⁺ = its complement `Cᶜ`
   594	      Deg1SupportedSlot d (foldResid d e p) j C (supportLayerOf p.conState) (foldRegion d e p) ∨
   595	        (∃ unit : (Fin (flatDim d) → ℝ) → ℝ,
   596	          ContinuousOn unit (foldRegion d e p) ∧ unit 0 ≠ 0 ∧
   597	            ∀ u ∈ foldRegion d e p, foldResid d e p j u = unit u))
   598	
   599	/-- **Subsumption (PIN 4 — PROVED rfl-class, never sorried): `FoldStepInvAt ⟹ LastLayerInv`.** Every slot
   600	takes the degree-1 (left) disjunct; definitional content wears no `sorry` (both consume the same
   601	`Deg1SupportedSlot` over the derived `D⁺`). -/
   602	theorem foldStepInvAt_to_lastLayerInv {N : ℕ} (d : Fin (N + 1) → ℕ)
   603	    (e : (Fin (flatDim d) → ℝ) ≃ₜ Tuple (k := ℝ) d) (C : Finset (Fin (flatDim d))) (p : TreePath d) :
   604	    FoldStepInvAt d e C p → LastLayerInv d e C p := by
   605	  rintro ⟨hSI, hDeg1⟩
   606	  exact ⟨hSI, fun j => Or.inl (hDeg1 j)⟩
   607	
   608	/-- **`GeneratorCleared`** (family ruling 2026-07-22, item 4 — the coordinator's consumer-fit pin, ONE named
   609	def so the two hand-copied ∃-shapes cannot drift). The fully-cleared-TERMINAL datum: a `StepInv` divisibility
   610	witness `q` whose generator-`i₀` combination is NONZERO at the origin — i.e. some generator has become a unit
   611	(the cleared-pivot datum `terminal_bezout` consumes). FALSE at last-layer ENTRY (the residual still vanishes
   612	at `0`); it is BUILT by `lastLayer_clear_preserves` at the `S = L` clears (units accumulate via the
   613	construction's Kronecker-diagonal `q`, alongside the slot-flip), NOT carried from entry. Its `∃ q` is LOCAL to
   614	this datum (a separate existential from the invariant's `∃ q`), so emitting it at the last layer does NOT pin
   615	the interior leaves' `q` — the interior `FoldStepInvAt` leaves never carry `GeneratorCleared`. -/
   616	def GeneratorCleared {N : ℕ} (d : Fin (N + 1) → ℕ) (e : (Fin (flatDim d) → ℝ) ≃ₜ Tuple (k := ℝ) d)
   617	    (p : TreePath d) : Prop :=
   618	  ∃ (i₀ : Fin (d (Fin.last N) * d 0))
   619	    (q : Fin (d (Fin.last N) * d 0) → Fin (foldNR d p) → (Fin (flatDim d) → ℝ) → ℝ),
   620	    StepInv (coreGen d e) (foldG d e p) (foldB d e p) (foldResid d e p) q (foldRegion d e p) ∧
   621	      ∑ j, q i₀ j 0 * foldResid d e p j 0 ≠ 0

   623	/-- **`DescendView`** (audit ruling 2026-07-22, A1 — the transition-law pin for the free `ed.nextState`).
   624	The STATE FRAGMENT of `IsRealBranch` (:775): `(ed.case, ed.nextState)` is a real oracle child of

   628	def DescendView {N : ℕ} (d : Fin (N + 1) → ℕ) (p : TreePath d) (ed : TreeEdge d p) : Prop :=
   629	  ∃ sc ∈ (conOracle d p.conState).stepChildren, sc.ecase = ed.case ∧ sc.child = ed.nextState

   631	/-- The flat coordinate of the diagonal corner `(S, J, J)` of the layer-`S` block; `none` off-cone
   632	(`S ≥ N` or `J` out of the block). `Option` handles totality with no nonempty junk. (Moved up from its
   633	former spot below `canonCenterOf` so the elder's (III) cleared-pivot clause in `ShearWithinCarveRaw`
   634	can reference it — definitional-order fix; `cornerToFlat` depends only on `flatDim`/`tupIdxEquiv`.) -/
   635	noncomputable def cornerToFlat (d : Fin (N + 1) → ℕ) (S J : ℕ) : Option (Fin (flatDim d)) :=
   636	  if hS : S < N then
   637	    let i : Fin N := ⟨S, hS⟩
   638	    if hr : J < d i.succ then
   639	      if hc : J < d i.castSucc then some (tupIdxEquiv d ⟨⟨i, ⟨J, hr⟩⟩, ⟨J, hc⟩⟩) else none
   640	    else none
   641	  else none

   643	/-- **`ShearWithinCarveRaw`** (seat-L4's (B) form, 2026-07-22, ONE site for the carve grade). The RAW
   644	displacement `shearφ` (not `blockShear shearφ`) on layers `≥ sl := supportLayerOf node`: (I) write-side —
   645	the displacement is ZERO there (`shearφ u i = 0`); (II) read-side — every displacement coord IGNORES those
   646	layers. (B) fixes the (A) `(II)`-unsatisfiability (old `blockShear = id` write-side vs unrestricted-`i`
   647	read-side). Parameterized by `node`/`shearφ` so both `ShearWithinCarve` (last edge) and `IsRealBranch`'s
   648	per-step shear-pin call it — ONE site for the carve grade. Vacuous at `lastLayer_clear` (`sl` exhausts). -/
   649	def ShearWithinCarveRaw {N : ℕ} (d : Fin (N + 1) → ℕ) (e : (Fin (flatDim d) → ℝ) ≃ₜ Tuple (k := ℝ) d)
   650	    (node : TreePath d) (shearφ : (Fin (flatDim d) → ℝ) → (Fin (flatDim d) → ℝ)) : Prop :=
   651	  let sl := supportLayerOf node.conState
   652	  let V := foldRegion d e node
   653	  (∀ ℓ : ℕ, sl ≤ ℓ → ∀ i ∈ layerCoords d ℓ, ∀ u ∈ V, shearφ u i = 0) ∧
   654	    (∀ ℓ : ℕ, sl ≤ ℓ → ∀ i, IgnoresCoords (fun u ↦ shearφ u i) (layerCoords d ℓ) V) ∧
   655	    -- (III) CLEARED-PIVOT protection (L6 (★), 2026-07-22): displacement vanishes on every divisor
   656	    -- birth-corner in the node's ledger — a deeper step's shear can't move an earlier pivot, so each
   657	    -- step's pivot is preserved by all deeper steps. Write-side / corners-only; canonShearOf never
   658	    -- writes a pivot corner (⟹ emittable); edgeShear = id at case11/rollover, trivial.
   659	    (∀ (k : Fin node.conState.numDiv) (i : Fin (flatDim d)),
   660	      cornerToFlat d (node.conState.divBirthCoord k).1 (node.conState.divBirthCoord k).2 = some i →
   661	        ∀ u ∈ V, shearφ u i = 0)
   662	
   663	/-- **`ShearWithinCarve`** — the last-edge specialization of `ShearWithinCarveRaw` (node = `p.extend ed`,
   664	displacement = `ed.shearφ`). Region = CHILD `foldRegion d e (p.extend ed)`. -/
   665	def ShearWithinCarve {N : ℕ} (d : Fin (N + 1) → ℕ) (e : (Fin (flatDim d) → ℝ) ≃ₜ Tuple (k := ℝ) d)
   666	    {p : TreePath d} (ed : TreeEdge d p) : Prop :=
   667	  ShearWithinCarveRaw d e (p.extend ed) ed.shearφ

   669	/-! ### The fold-realization predicate — combinatorial branch-membership (FIX 2; elder/coordinator (a))
   670	
   671	`FoldRealizes` is the SECOND provenance predicate L5 emits (beside `FoldProduced`): each chart's map
   672	`gmap c` IS the accumulated coordinate change `foldG` along a REAL root→leaf branch of
   673	`buildTree d (conOracle d) conRoot`, and that branch reaches the chart's leaf. "Real branch" is
   674	COMBINATORIAL: each step's `(case, nextState)` is realised by SOME oracle `StepChild` at the parent
   675	node's state (`ecase`/`child` matched; the child's `esubst` = `runLen`/`mergeIdx` rides along as the
   676	witness), and the terminal state emits the leaf. It reads NO `center`/`pivot`/`shearφ` — the oracle
   677	emits none (`ConState` is combinatorial; `StepChild.esubst.localSub = id` placeholder).
   678	
   679	**Severance taxonomy — nine free-field axes, one exemplar each** (elder guardrail, the L3/L4 note): a
   680	free field on a quantified structure is a severance axis, so the audit is per-FIELD.
   681	* CONTENT — the constant / `Σw²` spectator-support residuals — dies by the `foldResid p` pin (no free
   682	  residual to instantiate).
   683	* SIZE — the `|S|=1`, `sh=id` lazy witness — dies by the same pin.
   684	* PATH — the `n` charts all sharing ONE tree branch (the all-charts-one-path collision) — dies HERE:
   685	  distinct leaves force distinct combinatorial branches, so a surjective `leafOf` (`FoldProduced`) plus
   686	  `reachesLeaf` forbids the collision.
   687	* COORDINATE — every chart blowing up the SAME coordinate `x`, so all `gmap` images concentrate on `x`
   688	  and the ball's `y`-directions stay uncovered — DIES via the canonCenter pin (rung C follow-round): the
   689	  step `center`/`pivot` are pinned in `IsRealBranch` to `canonCenterOf`/`canonPivotOf`, functions of the
   690	  combinatorial `(layer, cleared, mergeIdx)`, so distinct real branches walk DISTINCT residual blocks —
   691	  the geometry can no longer concentrate on one coordinate. This LIFTS L7 bridge-free (statement TRUE;
   692	  proof sorried) — see `canonCenterOf` and `leafPath_compactCover`.
   693	* LAYER — a FREE `ed : TreeEdge` admits `N ≤ ed.nextState.layer` (terminal-reaching), where
   694	  `foldResid (p.extend ed) = fun _ ↦ 1` and `Deg1SupportedOn (const 1) C'` is FALSE, so the L3/L4
   695	  conclusion is unsatisfiable there (`terminal_deg1_gap`, seat-L4 probe) — dies via fix (a)/(2): the
   696	  interior guard `ed.nextState.layer + 1 < N` on `case1`/`case2_preserves_stepInv` restricts them to a
   697	  strictly-interior step, and `terminal_edge_stepInv` carries the terminal transition (bare unit `StepInv`).
   698	* LAYER-TERMINAL-RANGE — the S=L discriminator (fix (2), 6th axis): at the LAST layer the case leaves'
   699	  `Deg1SupportedOn` center-linear residual can no longer hold on a CLEARED slot (the cleared pivot's
   700	  residual is a unit, nonzero at `0`), so `FoldStepInvAt` is too rigid across the clears — dies via
   701	  `LastLayerInv`, whose per-slot center-linear ∨ unit disjunction ADMITS the S=L range;
   702	  `lastLayer_clear_preserves` carries it slot-by-slot (child guard `+ 1 = N`) and the interior guard
   703	  `+ 1 < N` excludes it from L3/L4.
   704	* ANCHOR-FORM (fix (2) (A), 7th) — a coefficient `c ≡ 1` (constant) satisfies the raw `IgnoresCoords`
   705	  (a constant ignores everything) yet does NOT self-propagate (its Let-block child is not center-linear) —
   706	  the weakest-that-HOLDS trap. Dies at `PerLayerDeg1From`: the per-layer total-degree ≤ 1 bound on the
   707	  RESIDUAL (not the coefficients) is what self-propagates — the retired `DeeperMultilinear`-coefficient
   708	  form subsumed into it. The support clause must be weakest-that-INDUCTS.
   709	* FRAME (fix (2) (A), 8th) — a legal shear `u_i ↦ u_i + u_pivot` (permitted by all four `TreeEdge` shear
   710	  fields, which forbid WRITING the pivot coord, not READING it) breaks a raw-center Deg1 independently of
   711	  any coefficient clause. Dies at the `foldResid`-prepared frame (FIX-RESID bakes the Let-block renaming,
   712	  so `foldResid` is the prepared object) + the `D⁺`-disjointness (`D⁺ = Cᶜ` is disjoint from the support
   713	  `C` by `disjoint_compl_right`). RENDER CHECKPOINT: whether EVERY pivot-READING shear is absorbed is
   714	  answered by the preservation proof; a residual pivot-read obstruction returns as a NAMED FINDING (drawer:
   715	  a pivot-INDEPENDENCE strengthening of the 4th `TreeEdge` field, fidelity-clean per thread-33 §3 — the
   716	  Schur shears never read exceptional coords). "Fix frames, not fences." (Sub-axis: a function-valued field
   717	  is audited for what it READS, not only what it WRITES.)
   718	* DEGREE-2 SCHUR (fix (2) (A), 9th regression witness) — the raw child residual carries the `C21·C12`
   719	  cross-term, which is DEGREE-2; a raw / entry-level `SupportedOn` weakening FAILS against it (guards the
   720	  over-division boundary). The `C21·C12` is a `u_pivot`-carrying residue a per-layer degree-≤1 bound cannot
   721	  absorb (`u_pivot` is exceptional, NOT deeper), and the Schur complement subtracts EXACTLY it — so the Schur
   722	  is not bookkeeping, it is the DEGREE-RESTORATION step (it re-expresses the degree-2 residual in the
   723	  per-layer degree-≤1 form `PerLayerDeg1From` carries).
   724	-/
   725	
   726	/-- The construction decision at a state terminates emitting leaf `l` (combinatorial leaf-match). -/
   727	def decisionEmitsLeaf {L : ℕ} {M : Fin (L + 1) → ℕ} {s : ConState L} :
   728	    ConDecision M s → LeafData M → Prop
   729	  | .terminal l' _, l => l' = l
   730	  | .step _ _ _ _ _, _ => False
   731	
   732	/-! ### canonCenter — the bridge-free canonical slot assignment (rung C follow-round; coordinator (a))
   733	
   734	The Aoyagi coordinate of a resolution step, reconstructed from the COMBINATORIAL state `(layer, cleared)`
   735	+ the edge's `mergeIdx` via `tupIdxEquiv` — NOT the engine `divCoord` (which the oracle never emits), so
   736	BRIDGE-FREE. `ConState.divBirthCoord` (the IMMUTABLE birth corner, the `d‴→d` stability) and
   737	`widthMinUpto` (the running-min row bound) are already carried by the engine. Pinning each edge's
   738	center/pivot to these canonical slots (in `IsRealBranch`) forces distinct real branches to walk DISTINCT
   739	residual blocks, which KILLS the all-charts-one-coordinate (COORDINATE-axis) severance and lifts L7
   740	bridge-free (statement TRUE; proof still sorried).
   741	
   742	Flat/Aoyagi orientation (load-bearing — the "column-remnant transpose"): a `tupIdx d` entry is
   743	`(layer i, row : Fin d_{i+1}, col : Fin d_i)`, but the engine's residual `(resRows, resCols)` (decision
   744	sites) are `resRows = widthMinUpto d layer − cleared` (running-min) and `resCols = d(layer+1) − cleared`
   745	(raw). So the engine `resRows` maps to the flat COL axis (`Fin d_layer`) and `resCols` to the flat ROW
   746	axis (`Fin d_{layer+1}`). Hence `canonCenter` caps the flat COL by `widthMinUpto` (running-min) and the
   747	flat ROW is the raw remnant (automatic in `Fin d_{layer+1}`). `|center| = resRows·resCols` = the birth
   748	`divExp`, so it is consistent with `FoldProduced.hstep_block` (`jexp = |center|−1`); `FoldProduced`'s
   749	`jac`/`divExp` are UNTOUCHED (the elder's accumulated `{9,8,4}` = birth block-sizes + merge additions;
   750	the `9,4,1` here are the birth block SIZES, a different quantity, consistent not contradictory). -/
   751	
   752	/-- The canonical pivot of an edge: a case-1(1) MERGE returns the REUSED divisor's IMMUTABLE birth corner
   753	`s.divBirthCoord mergeIdx` (discharge cond (1)); a case-1(2)/case-2 NEW pivot is the current
   754	`(layer, cleared, cleared)` corner (the diagonal, transpose-invariant); a rollover has no blow-up. -/
   755	noncomputable def canonPivotOf (d : Fin (N + 1) → ℕ) (s : ConState N) (sc : StepChild d s) :
   756	    Option (Fin (flatDim d)) :=
   757	  match sc.ecase with
   758	  | StepCase.case11 =>
   759	      if hm : sc.esubst.mergeIdx < s.numDiv then
   760	        let bc := s.divBirthCoord ⟨sc.esubst.mergeIdx, hm⟩
   761	        cornerToFlat d bc.1 bc.2
   762	      else none
   763	  | StepCase.case12 => cornerToFlat d s.layer s.cleared
   764	  | StepCase.case2 => cornerToFlat d s.layer s.cleared
   765	  | StepCase.rollover => none
   766	
   767	/-- The canonical center of an edge: the layer-`s.layer` residual BLOCK in flat coordinates — flat rows
   768	`≥ cleared` (the raw column-remnant, automatic in `Fin d_{layer+1}`) and flat cols
   769	`∈ [cleared, widthMinUpto d layer)` (the running-min ROW bound, discharge cond (2); note the transpose).
   770	`∅` for a rollover (no blow-up) / off-cone. -/
   771	noncomputable def canonCenterOf (d : Fin (N + 1) → ℕ) (s : ConState N) (sc : StepChild d s) :
   772	    Finset (Fin (flatDim d)) :=
   773	  match sc.ecase with
   774	  | StepCase.rollover => ∅

because the divisibility engine it consumes lives downstream of `MonumentAtlas`. The dispatch is
verified correct; the two frontier obligations are named holes (elder-ruling-pending). -/

/-- **FRONTIER OBLIGATION — case11 δ=1 BOOST-READINESS** (Codex xhigh 2026-07-22, elder-ruling-pending).
For a real case1(1) edge at δ=1, the parent residual is `Deg1SupportedOn` the LEDGER (boost) center
`ed.center = {pivot} ∪ partial-block` — even though its GEOMETRIC support `supportAt` is the larger
full layer block: the untouched (`support ∖ center`) terms carry `u_pivot` in their non-dominant
b-chain coefficient `b_i/b_1`. NOT derivable from `hinv`'s `supportAt`-`Deg1SupportedSlot` nor from
`IsRealBranch` as pinned (Codex); the honest home (new carried-invariant conjunct / stronger
`IsRealBranch` shear pin / this stub) is the elder's call, gated on the boost-readiness battery on
(2,2,2,2)/(3,3,2,2). Feeds `stepInv_child_delta1_append` exactly like the case12 cover route. -/

    (hδ : edgeδ d p = true) (hc11 : ed.case = StepCase.case11)
    (hbranch : (p.extend ed).IsRealBranch e) :
    Deg1SupportedOn (foldResid d e p) ed.center (foldRegion d e p) := by

/-- **Conjunct A (divisibility ∃q) of the wall — dispatched.** δ=0 → `stepInv_child_delta0`
(case-generic pullback, BANKED); δ=1 case12 → `stepInv_child_delta1_append` fed
`deg1SupportedOn_center_of_hslot` (cover route, BANKED); δ=1 case11 → `stepInv_child_delta1_append` fed
`realBranch_boostReady_case11` (the boost-center Deg1 obligation). The SAME append lemma closes both δ=1
sub-cases — only the `Deg1SupportedOn ed.center` source differs. -/
theorem case1_conjA (d : Fin (N + 1) → ℕ)
    (e : (Fin (flatDim d) → ℝ) ≃ₜ Tuple (k := ℝ) d)
    (p : TreePath d) (ed : TreeEdge d p) (hcase1 : ed.isCase1)
    (hlayer : ed.nextState.layer + 1 < N)
    (hinv : FoldStepInvAt d e (supportAt d p.conState.layer p.conState.cleared) p)
    (hbranch : (p.extend ed).IsRealBranch e) :
    ∃ q : Fin (d (Fin.last N) * d 0) → Fin (foldNR d (p.extend ed)) → (Fin (flatDim d) → ℝ) → ℝ,
      StepInv (coreGen d e) (foldG d e (p.extend ed)) (foldB d e (p.extend ed))
        (foldResid d e (p.extend ed)) q (foldRegion d e (p.extend ed)) := by
  classical
  have hlt : ¬ N ≤ ed.nextState.layer := Nat.not_le.mpr (by omega)
  obtain ⟨q, hq⟩ := hinv.1
  by_cases hδ : edgeδ d p = true
  · rcases hcase1 with hc11 | hc12
    · -- δ=1 case11: boost-readiness → append crux
      exact stepInv_child_delta1_append d e ed hlt hδ
        (realBranch_boostReady_case11 d e ed hδ hc11 hbranch) q hq
    · -- δ=1 case12: cover route → append crux
      exact stepInv_child_delta1_append d e ed hlt hδ
        (deg1SupportedOn_center_of_hslot d e ed hδ (Or.inl hc12) hbranch hinv.2) q hq
  · -- δ=0: pure pullback (case-generic)
    have hδ0 : edgeδ d p = false := by
      cases h : edgeδ d p with
      | false => rfl
      | true => exact absurd h hδ
    exact stepInv_child_delta0 d e ed hlt hδ0 q hq

/-- **⟨THE WALL — primed⟩** `case1_preserves_stepInv'`, statement-identical to
`MonumentAtlas.case1_preserves_stepInv`. Conjunct A is `case1_conjA` (dispatched; consumes the
boost-readiness obligation for the δ=1 case11 sub-branch); conjunct B is the shared child
`Deg1SupportedSlot` descent — the single tracked `sorry` (closes to the node-form
`realBranch_multiAffine (p.extend ed) hbranch` when the elder's node-form micro-round bakes; identical
to `Case2Wire.case2_preserves_stepInv'`'s conjunct B). -/
theorem case1_preserves_stepInv'
    (d : Fin (N + 1) → ℕ) (hN : 0 < N) (hpos : ∀ k, 0 < d k)
    (e : (Fin (flatDim d) → ℝ) ≃ₜ Tuple (k := ℝ) d)
    (p : TreePath d) (ed : TreeEdge d p) (hcase1 : ed.isCase1)
    (hlayer : ed.nextState.layer + 1 < N)
    (hinv : FoldStepInvAt d e (supportAt d p.conState.layer p.conState.cleared) p)
    (hbranch : (p.extend ed).IsRealBranch e) :
    FoldStepInvAt d e
      (supportAt d (p.extend ed).conState.layer (p.extend ed).conState.cleared) (p.extend ed) := by
  refine ⟨case1_conjA d e p ed hcase1 hlayer hinv hbranch, ?_⟩
  -- CONJUNCT B — child `Deg1SupportedSlot` on `supportAt(child)` (the descended block); shared with
  -- case2. map: B-L4-case1-conjB (node-form realBranch_multiAffine (p.extend ed) hbranch when baked).
  sorry

end DLNFibre.DLN.Aoyagi

lean/DLNFibre/DLN/RLCT/Engine/O5Realization.lean:792:              (Fin.last s.numDiv) = s.cleared := fun e => by
   130	theorem tildeOf_le {L : ℕ} {T : Fin L → ℕ} (i : Fin L) : tildeOf T ≤ T i := by
   131	  have hL : 0 < L := lt_of_le_of_lt (Nat.zero_le i.val) i.isLt
   132	  haveI : Nonempty (Fin L) := ⟨i⟩
   133	  simp only [tildeOf, dif_pos hL]
   134	  exact Finset.inf'_le _ (Finset.mem_univ i)
   135	
   136	/-- `c ≤ tildeOf T` from a uniform lower bound `c ≤ T i` (companion to `tildeOf_le`). -/
   137	theorem le_tildeOf {L : ℕ} {T : Fin L → ℕ} (hL : 0 < L) {c : ℕ} (h : ∀ i, c ≤ T i) :
   138	    c ≤ tildeOf T := by
   139	  simp only [tildeOf, dif_pos hL]
   140	  exact Finset.le_inf' _ _ (fun i _ => h i)
   141	
   142	/-- **The clearing level drops to `J` after a tail-write** (the case-1(1) merge fact): if there is a
   143	tail coordinate (`layer < L`), then `t̃ = min (setTail layer J T) ≤ J` — the tail index `L−1`
   144	carries value `J`. The load-bearing bound of the reworked `pendingCount` descent. -/
   145	theorem tildeOf_setTail_le {L : ℕ} {layer cleared : ℕ} {T : Fin L → ℕ} (h : layer < L) :
   146	    tildeOf (setTail layer cleared T) ≤ cleared := by
   147	  have hle : layer ≤ (⟨L - 1, by omega⟩ : Fin L).val := by change layer ≤ L - 1; omega
   148	  have hval : setTail layer cleared T ⟨L - 1, by omega⟩ = cleared := by
   149	    simp only [setTail]; rw [if_pos hle]
   150	  exact le_of_le_of_eq (tildeOf_le ⟨L - 1, by omega⟩) hval
   151	
   152	/-- **A tail-write preserves weak-decrease** given the head is weak-decreasing and dominates the
   153	written tail value `cleared`: `setTail layer cleared T` is antitone. The head (`p < layer`) keeps
   154	`T` (weak-dec by `hwd`); the tail (`p ≥ layer`) is the constant `cleared`, `≤` the head by `hc`. The
   155	common engine of the case-1(1) and case-2 weak-decrease preservation. -/
   156	theorem setTail_antitone {L : ℕ} {layer cleared : ℕ} {T : Fin L → ℕ}
   157	    (hwd : ∀ i j : Fin L, i ≤ j → T j ≤ T i)
   158	    (hc : ∀ p : Fin L, (p : ℕ) < layer → cleared ≤ T p)
   159	    {a b : Fin L} (hab : a ≤ b) :
   160	    setTail layer cleared T b ≤ setTail layer cleared T a := by
   161	  have hab' : (a : ℕ) ≤ (b : ℕ) := hab
   162	  simp only [setTail]
   163	  split_ifs with hb ha
   164	  · exact le_refl _
   165	  · exact hc a (by omega)
   166	  · omega
   167	  · exact hwd a b hab
   168	
   169	/-! ## The per-case transitions + μ-descent (rung 2B)
   170	
   171	Each of the construction's four step kinds is a `ConState → ConState` map, and each strictly drops a
   172	μ component under its step precondition — so `conRel (step s) s` holds and the recursion is
   173	well-founded. The KILL-CONDITION (a step decreasing NO component ⇒ design break) is CLEARED: every
   174	transition below drops a component. `stepAppendAdvance` covers BOTH case-1(2) and case-2 (they
   175	differ only in the appended exponent, which μ does not read) — both drop component 2. -/
   176	
   177	/-- **case-1(1) merge transition**: mutate divisor `i`'s rank-pattern via the `T`-rule tail-write
   178	(`setTail`), so its derived clearing level drops to `J`; the ledger is otherwise unchanged (the
   179	exponent bump does not affect μ). -/
   180	def ConState.stepCase11 {L : ℕ} (s : ConState L) (i : Fin s.numDiv) : ConState L :=
   181	  ⟨s.layer, s.cleared, s.numDiv, s.divExp,
   182	    Function.update s.divProfile i (setTail s.layer s.cleared (s.divProfile i)),
   183	    s.numGen, s.genDivExp, s.divBirthCoord⟩
   184	
   185	/-- **case-1(2)/case-2 transition**: append a new divisor of exponent `e` and (tail-written)
   186	rank-pattern `t₀`, then advance the cleared count by one. The two cases differ ONLY in `e`/`t₀`,
   187	which μ does not read — both drop component 2. The new divisor's `genDivExp` column is `0`
   188	(placeholder; real sharing propagation is T4). -/
   189	def ConState.stepAppendAdvance {L : ℕ} (s : ConState L) (e : ℕ) (t₀ : Fin L → ℕ) : ConState L :=
   190	  ⟨s.layer, s.cleared + 1, s.numDiv + 1, Fin.snoc s.divExp e,
   191	    Fin.snoc s.divProfile (setTail s.layer s.cleared t₀),
   192	    s.numGen, fun g => Fin.snoc (s.genDivExp g) 0,
   193	    Fin.snoc s.divBirthCoord (s.layer, s.cleared)⟩
   194	
   195	/-- **Layer rollover transition**: advance the layer (`S → S+1`) and reset the cleared count
   196	(`J → 0`); the divisor ledger carries over. -/
   197	def ConState.stepRollover {L : ℕ} (s : ConState L) : ConState L :=
   198	  ⟨s.layer + 1, 0, s.numDiv, s.divExp, s.divProfile, s.numGen, s.genDivExp, s.divBirthCoord⟩
   199	
   200	/-- A case-1(1) merge strictly drops the pending-divisor count: its target divisor `i` (pending,
   201	`J < t̃_i`) has its rank-pattern tail-written, so the derived `t̃_i` drops to `≤ J` (needs a tail
   202	coordinate, `layer < L`), leaving the pending set with `i` erased. (In the construction the
   203	precondition `J < t̃_i` is the eligibility conjunct `t̃_i = J + runLen`, `runLen ≥ 1`.) -/
   204	theorem pendingCount_stepCase11_lt {L : ℕ} (s : ConState L) (i : Fin s.numDiv)
   205	    (hlayer : s.layer < L) (hi : s.cleared < s.divTilde i) :
   206	    (s.stepCase11 i).pendingCount < s.pendingCount := by
   207	  set upd := Function.update s.divProfile i (setTail s.layer s.cleared (s.divProfile i)) with hupd
   208	  have hnp : tildeOf (upd i) ≤ s.cleared := by
   209	    rw [hupd, Function.update_self]; exact tildeOf_setTail_le hlayer
   210	  have hset : (Finset.univ.filter (fun k : Fin s.numDiv => s.cleared < tildeOf (upd k)))
   211	      = (Finset.univ.filter (fun k : Fin s.numDiv => s.cleared < tildeOf (s.divProfile k))).erase i
   212	      := by
   213	    ext k
   214	    simp only [Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_univ, true_and, Finset.mem_erase]
   215	    by_cases hk : k = i
   216	    · subst hk
   217	      simp only [ne_eq, not_true_eq_false, false_and, iff_false, not_lt]
   218	      exact hnp
   219	    · rw [hupd, Function.update_of_ne hk]; tauto
   220	  have hmem : i ∈ Finset.univ.filter
   221	      (fun k : Fin s.numDiv => s.cleared < tildeOf (s.divProfile k)) :=
   222	    Finset.mem_filter.mpr ⟨Finset.mem_univ i, hi⟩
   223	  -- One DecidablePred synthesis point (matching `hset`); the goal reduces to this LHS by defeq
   224	  -- (`(stepCase11).divTilde k = tildeOf ((stepCase11).divProfile k)`, `divProfile = upd`).
   225	  have hlt : (Finset.univ.filter (fun k : Fin s.numDiv => s.cleared < tildeOf (upd k))).card
   226	      < (Finset.univ.filter
   227	          (fun k : Fin s.numDiv => s.cleared < tildeOf (s.divProfile k))).card := by
   228	    rw [hset]; exact Finset.card_erase_lt_of_mem hmem
   229	  exact hlt
   230	
   231	/-- **case-1(1) drops μ (component 3).** Given the eligible (pending) target, `conRel` holds — the
   232	layer and cleared count are unchanged, so μ₁, μ₂ tie and μ₃ (`pendingCount`) drops. -/
   233	theorem conRel_stepCase11 (M : Fin (L + 1) → ℕ) (s : ConState L) (i : Fin s.numDiv)
   234	    (hlayer : s.layer < L) (hi : s.cleared < s.divTilde i) :
   235	    conRel M (s.stepCase11 i) s := by
   236	  unfold conRel conMeasure
   237	  rw [Prod.Lex.toLex_lt_toLex]
   238	  refine Or.inr ⟨rfl, ?_⟩
   239	  rw [Prod.Lex.toLex_lt_toLex]
   240	  exact Or.inr ⟨rfl, pendingCount_stepCase11_lt s i hlayer hi⟩
   241	
   242	/-- **case-1(2)/case-2 drops μ (component 2).** Advancing `J` by one strictly drops `layerCap − J`
   243	(the precondition `J < layerCap` gives room); the layer ties, so μ₁ ties and μ₂ drops. -/
   244	theorem conRel_stepAppendAdvance (M : Fin (L + 1) → ℕ) (s : ConState L) (e : ℕ) (t₀ : Fin L → ℕ)
   245	    (hlt : s.cleared < layerCap M) :
   246	    conRel M (s.stepAppendAdvance e t₀) s := by
   247	  unfold conRel conMeasure
   248	  rw [Prod.Lex.toLex_lt_toLex]
   249	  refine Or.inr ⟨rfl, ?_⟩
   250	  rw [Prod.Lex.toLex_lt_toLex]
   251	  refine Or.inl ?_
   252	  change layerCap M - (s.cleared + 1) < layerCap M - s.cleared
   253	  omega
   254	
   255	/-- **Layer rollover drops μ (component 1).** Advancing `S` by one strictly drops `L+1 − S` (the
   256	precondition `S ≤ L` gives room); μ₁ drops, so μ₂/μ₃ may reset freely. -/
   257	theorem conRel_stepRollover (M : Fin (L + 1) → ℕ) (s : ConState L) (hlayer : s.layer ≤ L) :
   258	    conRel M s.stepRollover s := by
   259	  unfold conRel conMeasure
   260	  rw [Prod.Lex.toLex_lt_toLex]
  1860	  simpa using of_decide_eq_true this
  1861	
  1862	/-- **The o4 landing pad**: on a `SameLevelChainInv` state, IF some divisor sits at level `target`,
  1863	the chooser does NOT fall back — a componentwise-min exists (the same-level chain makes `def4_min`
  1864	total). PROVED below (`chooserTotalOnChain_of_sameLevel`) — the o2 min-existence half of the
  1865	mutual induction. -/
  1866	def ChooserTotalOnChain {L : ℕ} (s : ConState L) : Prop :=
  1867	  ∀ target : ℕ, (∃ k : Fin s.numDiv, s.divTilde k = target) → (chooseMin s target).isSome
  1868	
  1869	/-- **On a chain, a componentwise minimum equals a sum minimum**: if `a`, `b` are Def-4-comparable
  1870	and `∑ a ≤ ∑ b`, then `a ≤ b` componentwise. (If `b ≤ a` but `a ≰ b`, some coord is strict, so
  1871	`∑ b < ∑ a` — contradicting `∑ a ≤ ∑ b`.) The scalar `∑` witnesses the chain minimum. -/
  1872	theorem le_of_comparable_sum_le {L : ℕ} {a b : Fin L → ℕ}
  1873	    (hcomp : (∀ j, a j ≤ b j) ∨ (∀ j, b j ≤ a j))
  1874	    (hsum : ∑ j, a j ≤ ∑ j, b j) : ∀ j, a j ≤ b j := by
  1875	  rcases hcomp with h | h
  1876	  · exact h
  1877	  · by_contra hcon
  1878	    push Not at hcon
  1879	    obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := hcon
  1880	    have hlt : ∑ i, b i < ∑ i, a i :=
  1881	      Finset.sum_lt_sum (fun i _ => h i) ⟨j, Finset.mem_univ j, hj⟩
  1882	    omega
  1883	
  1884	/-- **`ChooserTotalOnChain` PROVED from `SameLevelChainInv`** (o2 min-existence). The eligible set
  1885	`{k : t̃ k = target}` is same-level, hence a chain (`hchain`); a `∑`-minimum over it (finite,
  1886	nonempty) is therefore a componentwise minimum (`le_of_comparable_sum_le`), which is exactly
  1887	`chooseMin`'s `find?` predicate — so the chooser returns it (`isSome`), never the fallback. -/
  1888	theorem chooserTotalOnChain_of_sameLevel {L : ℕ} (s : ConState L)
  1889	    (hchain : SameLevelChainInv s) : ChooserTotalOnChain s := by
  1890	  rintro target ⟨k₀, hk₀⟩
  1891	  obtain ⟨k, hkE, hkmin⟩ := Finset.exists_min_image
  1892	    (Finset.univ.filter (fun k => s.divTilde k = target))
  1893	    (fun k => ∑ j, s.divProfile k j)
  1894	    ⟨k₀, Finset.mem_filter.mpr ⟨Finset.mem_univ k₀, hk₀⟩⟩
  1895	  rw [Finset.mem_filter] at hkE
  1896	  have hpred : s.divTilde k = target ∧ ∀ k' : Fin s.numDiv, s.divTilde k' = target →
  1897	      ∀ j : Fin L, s.divProfile k j ≤ s.divProfile k' j := by
  1898	    refine ⟨hkE.2, fun k' hk' j => ?_⟩
  1899	    have hcomp := hchain k k' (hkE.2.trans hk'.symm)
  1900	    have hsum : ∑ i, s.divProfile k i ≤ ∑ i, s.divProfile k' i :=
  1901	      hkmin k' (Finset.mem_filter.mpr ⟨Finset.mem_univ k', hk'⟩)
  1902	    exact le_of_comparable_sum_le hcomp hsum j
  1903	  rw [Option.isSome_iff_ne_none, Ne, chooseMin, List.find?_eq_none]
  1904	  push Not
  1905	  exact ⟨k, List.mem_finRange k, by simpa using hpred⟩
  1906	
  1907	/-! ## o2: the dispatch `classify` (simulator `_proc`, indexing pinned `layer = S−1`)
  1908	
  1909	Which step the state admits — the simulator's dispatch, transcribed with `layer = S−1`:
  1910	`S = layer+1`, `Mrun(S) = min(M i : i ≤ layer) = widthMinUpto layer`, `MSp1 = min(Mrun(S), M⁽ˢ⁺¹⁾) =
  1911	widthMinUpto (layer+1)`. Terminal at `layer = L` (`S = L+1`); else rollover if `J ≥ MSp1`; else
  1912	case-1 on the least occupied `t̃`-level in `[J+1, Mrun(S)−1]`; else case-2. Pure/decidable — the
  1913	CONE-GOODNESS (this dispatch makes μ-progress on invariant states) is proven separately. -/
  1914	
  1915	/-- Which step a live state admits (the dispatch tag; `case1` carries the target clearing level). -/
  1916	inductive StepKind where
  1917	  | terminal
  1918	  | rollover
  1919	  | case1 (target : ℕ)
  1920	  | case2
  1921	  deriving DecidableEq, Repr
  1922	
  1923	/-- **The dispatch** (simulator `_proc`, `layer = S−1`): the step kind the state admits. -/
  1924	def classify (M : Fin (L + 1) → ℕ) (s : ConState L) : StepKind :=
  1925	  if L ≤ s.layer then .terminal
  1926	  else if widthMinUpto M (s.layer + 1) ≤ s.cleared then .rollover
  1927	  else
  1928	    let occ := (List.finRange s.numDiv).filterMap (fun k =>
  1929	      let t := s.divTilde k
  1930	      if s.cleared + 1 ≤ t ∧ t + 1 ≤ widthMinUpto M s.layer then some t else none)
  1931	    match occ.min? with
  1932	    | some target => .case1 target
  1933	    | none => .case2
  1934	
  1935	/-! ## o2: the per-branch step decisions (the oracle's emissions; assembled into the total oracle)
  1936	
  1937	Each branch of `classify` emits a `ConDecision`. The rollover branch is the first and simplest — a
  1938	single chartless edge; it lands directly on the ratified carrier (`StepCase.rollover` + the
  1939	at-exhaustion guard). The case-1/case-2 branches (with the chooser + the divExp-bump child) follow. -/
  1940	
  1941	/-- **The rollover step decision**: at an at-exhaustion state (`widthMinUpto M (s.layer+1) ≤
  1942	s.cleared`, with `s.layer ≤ L` for the descent) the oracle emits ONE chartless `rollover` edge to
  1943	`s.stepRollover` (layer `S+1`, `J := 0`). The node is `s.toStepData` (residual dims unused by a
  1944	relabel edge, so `0`); the child ledger is `stepUpdate … rollover` (rfl); the eligibility clause is
  1945	vacuous (`rollover ∉ {case11,case12}`) and the at-exhaustion guard is exactly `hex`. -/
  1946	noncomputable def rolloverDecision {L : ℕ} (M : Fin (L + 1) → ℕ) (s : ConState L)
  1947	    (hlayer : s.layer ≤ L) (hex : widthMinUpto M (s.layer + 1) ≤ s.cleared) : ConDecision M s :=
  1948	  .step (s.toStepData M 0 0)
  1949	    [⟨StepCase.rollover, ⟨id, 0, 0, 0, Fin.elim0⟩, s.stepRollover,
  1950	      conRel_stepRollover M s hlayer⟩]
  1951	    rfl rfl
  1952	    (by
  1953	      intro c hc
  1954	      simp only [List.mem_singleton] at hc
  1955	      subst hc
  1956	      refine ⟨rfl, ?_, ?_⟩
  1957	      · rintro (h | h) <;> nomatch h
  1958	      · intro _; exact hex)
  1959	
  1960	/-- **The case-2 step decision**: append a new full-block pivot (exponent `resRows·resCols`, head
  1961	reset to `runMinWidth`, tail `J`) and advance `J`. The child is definitionally
  1962	`s.stepAppendAdvance (resRows·resCols) runMinWidth` — its ledger IS `stepUpdate … case2` (rfl).
  1963	Eligibility and the rollover guard are both vacuous (case-2). Needs the room `hcap : cleared <
  1964	layerCap` for the μ₂ descent. -/
  1965	noncomputable def case2Decision {L : ℕ} (M : Fin (L + 1) → ℕ) (s : ConState L)
  1966	    (resRows resCols : ℕ) (hcap : s.cleared < layerCap M) : ConDecision M s :=
  1967	  .step (s.toStepData M resRows resCols)
  1968	    [⟨StepCase.case2, ⟨id, 0, 0, 0, Fin.elim0⟩,
  1969	      s.stepAppendAdvance (resRows * resCols) (fun p => runMinWidth M p),
  1970	      conRel_stepAppendAdvance M s (resRows * resCols) (fun p => runMinWidth M p) hcap⟩]
  1971	    rfl rfl
  1972	    (by
  1973	      intro c hc
  1974	      simp only [List.mem_singleton] at hc
  1975	      subst hc
  1976	      refine ⟨rfl, ?_, ?_⟩
  1977	      · rintro (h | h) <;> nomatch h
  1978	      · intro h; nomatch h)
  1979	
  1980	/-- **The case-1(2) split step decision**: split the chosen divisor `f` into a new pivot with
  1981	INHERITED head (`t₀ = s.divProfile f`), exponent `s.divExp f + runLen·resCols`, tail `J`; advance
  1982	`J`. The child is `s.stepAppendAdvance (s.divExp f + runLen·resCols) (s.divProfile f)`; its ledger
  1983	IS `stepUpdate … case12` (the split reads `divExp/divProfile mergeIdx`, in range as `f.isLt`). The
  1984	eligibility conjunct is `helig : s.divTilde f = s.cleared + runLen`; the rollover guard is vacuous. -/
  1985	noncomputable def case12Decision {L : ℕ} (M : Fin (L + 1) → ℕ) (s : ConState L)
  1986	    (f : Fin s.numDiv) (runLen resRows resCols : ℕ) (hcap : s.cleared < layerCap M)
  1987	    (helig : s.divTilde f = s.cleared + runLen) : ConDecision M s :=
  1988	  .step (s.toStepData M resRows resCols)
  1989	    [⟨StepCase.case12, ⟨id, runLen, f.val, 0, Fin.elim0⟩,
  1990	      s.stepAppendAdvance (s.divExp f + runLen * resCols) (s.divProfile f),
  1991	      conRel_stepAppendAdvance M s (s.divExp f + runLen * resCols) (s.divProfile f) hcap⟩]
  1992	    rfl rfl
  1993	    (by
  1994	      intro c hc
  1995	      simp only [List.mem_singleton] at hc
  1996	      subst hc
  1997	      refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  1998	      · show (s.stepAppendAdvance (s.divExp f + runLen * resCols) (s.divProfile f)).toRootLedger
  1999	            = stepUpdate (s.toStepData M resRows resCols) StepCase.case12 ⟨id, runLen, f.val, 0,
  2000	              Fin.elim0⟩
   130	def ChartBridge (M : Fin (L + 1) → ℕ) (t : ResolutionTree M) : Prop :=
   131	  ∃ atlas : List (LeafData M),
   132	    (∃ U : Set (Params M), IsOpen U ∧ (0 : Params M) ∈ U ∧
   133	        U ⊆ ⋃ c ∈ atlas, c.chartMap '' c.srcBox) ∧
   134	    (∀ c ∈ atlas,
   135	      MeasurableSet c.srcBox ∧
   136	        (∃ R : ℝ, 0 < R ∧ c.srcBox ⊆ ⇑(paramsEquivFlat M) ⁻¹' cubeBox (flatDim M) R) ∧
   137	        Function.Injective c.divCoord ∧ Function.Injective c.resCoord ∧
   138	        Disjoint (Set.range c.divCoord) (Set.range c.resCoord) ∧
   139	        (∃ N : Set (Params M), volume N = 0 ∧ Set.InjOn c.chartMap (c.srcBox \ N)) ∧
   140	        LeafPullback c ∧ LeafJacobian c) ∧
   141	    (∀ c ∈ atlas, (∀ k : Fin c.numDiv, c.divExp k ∈ ResolutionTree.terminalExponents t) ∧
   142	      (0 < c.resRank → c.resRank ∈ ResolutionTree.terminalExponents t))
   143	
   144	/-- **The running-min corank** `M(i+1) = min(M⁽¹⁾ … M⁽ⁱ⁺¹⁾)` at head index `p` (0-indexed, so the
   145	1-indexed layer `i = p+1`): the min of the widths `M 0 … M p.succ`. FIX-A (below) caps the Case-2
   146	head-reset at this, not the RAW `M p.succ`. -/
   147	def runMinWidth (M : Fin (L + 1) → ℕ) (p : Fin L) : ℕ :=
   148	  (Finset.Iic p.succ).inf' ⟨p.succ, Finset.mem_Iic.mpr le_rfl⟩ M
   149	
   150	/-- `min(M i : i ≤ n)` — the running-min width through paper layer `n+1` (`= Mrun(n+1)`; it is
   151	`Mrun(S)` at `n = layer = S−1`). Nonempty (index `0` qualifies), so a `Finset.inf'`. Lives here (not
   152	`EngineConstruction`) because the rollover-edge at-exhaustion guard in `StepRel` below reads it — the
   153	guard is pinned to the construction dispatch's rollover trigger `widthMinUpto (layer+1) ≤ cleared`. -/
   154	def widthMinUpto (M : Fin (L + 1) → ℕ) (n : ℕ) : ℕ :=
   155	  (Finset.univ.filter (fun i : Fin (L + 1) => (i : ℕ) ≤ n)).inf' ⟨0, by simp⟩ M
   156	
   157	/-- **The typed transition** (rung 1, fork 9): the child root ledger CORE a step DETERMINES from its
   158	parent `n`, the `case`, and the edge substitution `σ` — `numDiv`, per-divisor `divExp`/`divTilde`,
   159	`cleared`. Faithful to the page-image transitions:
   160	* **case 1(1)** — merge INTO divisor `σ.mergeIdx`: its exponent `+= runLen·resCols`
   161	  (`M' = M + J₁·(M^{(S+1)}−J)`, preprint p.16), its `t̃ → cleared`; `numDiv`/`cleared` unchanged.
   162	* **case 1(2)** — SPLIT divisor `σ.mergeIdx` into a NEW pivot appended at the end with exponent
   163	  `divExp(mergeIdx) + runLen·resCols` and `t̃ = cleared` (preprint p.17 `M'_{S,J+1} = M_{sk} +
   164	  J₁·(M^{(S+1)}−J)`, `t̃_{S,J+1}=J`); advance `cleared` by one.
   165	* **case 2** — append a NEW pivot divisor of exponent `resRows·resCols` (preprint p.20) with
   166	  `t̃ = cleared`; advance `cleared` by one, like case 1(2) (elder p.21: J increases by ONE per
   167	  Case-2 step — the full block clears over SUCCESSIVE Case-2 steps, each a distinct pivot of
   168	  strictly smaller exponent `(M(S)−J−i)(M^{(S+1)}−J−i)`; a `+= resRows` fast-forward would DROP
   169	  those divisors, one of which can be the binding minimum).
   170	SUPPORT PROPAGATION is STOP-AND-SURFACEd (NOT modelled here): transporting the `support` `Finset`s
   171	across the per-divisor re-indexing balloons (Codex-confirmed; compass second cost center), deferred
   172	to the named `genDivExp` redesign rung. The transitions are page-pinned to the AOYAGI preprint
   173	(`aoyagi-2023-neural-networks-preprint.pdf`, NOT Lehalleur-Rimányi): case-1(1) merge p.16; case-1(2)
   174	exponent p.17; case-2 exponent p.20 + `cleared+1` p.21; case-1 eligibility p.15. -/
   175	def stepUpdate {M : Fin (L + 1) → ℕ} (n : StepData M) (c : StepCase) (σ : ChartSubst M) :
   176	    ResolutionTree.RootLedger L :=
   177	  -- `T`-rule tail-write (rung R1, pnp-atlas verdict 3): the tail `t⁽ˢ⁾…⁽ᴸ⁾ := J`. Fixed index set
   178	  -- `{1..L}`, no re-indexing. INDEXING (trace-`S` 1-indexed ↔ Lean `layer` 0-indexed): tail iff
   179	  -- `n.layer ≤ p.val`. The head is per-case (unchanged / inherited / width-reset).
   180	  let setTail : (Fin L → ℕ) → (Fin L → ℕ) :=
   181	    fun T p => if n.layer ≤ (p : ℕ) then n.cleared else T p
   182	  match c with
   183	  | StepCase.case11 =>
   184	      -- mutate the fixed divisor `mergeIdx`: head UNCHANGED, tail `:= J` (Aoyagi p.16)
   185	      { numDiv := n.numDiv
   186	        divExp := fun k => if (k : ℕ) = σ.mergeIdx then n.divExp k + σ.runLen * n.resCols
   187	                            else n.divExp k
   188	        divProfile := fun k => if (k : ℕ) = σ.mergeIdx then setTail (n.divProfile k)
   189	                                else n.divProfile k
   190	        cleared := n.cleared }
   191	  | StepCase.case12 =>
   192	      -- new pivot: head INHERITED from the parent divisor `mergeIdx`, tail `:= J` (Aoyagi p.17)
   193	      { numDiv := n.numDiv + 1
   194	        divExp := Fin.snoc n.divExp
   195	          ((if h : σ.mergeIdx < n.numDiv then n.divExp ⟨σ.mergeIdx, h⟩ else 0)
   196	            + σ.runLen * n.resCols)
   197	        divProfile := Fin.snoc n.divProfile
   198	          (setTail (if h : σ.mergeIdx < n.numDiv then n.divProfile ⟨σ.mergeIdx, h⟩ else fun _ => 0))
   199	        cleared := n.cleared + 1 }
   200	  | StepCase.case2 =>
   201	      -- new pivot: head RESET to the RUNNING-MIN width `t⁽ⁱ⁾ := M(i+1) = min(M⁽¹⁾…M⁽ⁱ⁺¹⁾)`
   202	      -- (`runMinWidth`), tail `:= J`. DEVIATION-FROM-PAGE (FIX-A): Aoyagi p.20 prints the RAW
   203	      -- `M⁽ⁱ⁺¹⁾`, but that label disagrees with the SAME step's running-min exponent at
   204	      -- non-monotone widths (label≠exponent at (2,2,3,2); a verified defect, Def-3's class — see
   205	      -- theory/aoyagi-2023-reproduction/verify-case2-rawwidth-defect.md). The cap restores
   206	      -- label==exponent + an Adm-clean t̃=0 atlas; invisible at monotone widths / L ≤ 2.
   207	      { numDiv := n.numDiv + 1
   208	        divExp := Fin.snoc n.divExp (n.resRows * n.resCols)
   209	        divProfile := Fin.snoc n.divProfile (setTail (fun p => runMinWidth M p))
   210	        cleared := n.cleared + 1 }
   211	  | StepCase.rollover =>
   212	      -- LAYER ROLLOVER (Aoyagi p.21 block-exhaustion reindexing `S → S+1`): a CHARTLESS relabel. The
   213	      -- divisor ledger (`numDiv`/`divExp`/`divProfile`) carries over UNCHANGED; only the per-layer
   214	      -- cleared count resets (`J := 0`). No coordinate change (the pure-(a) rollover gauge,
   215	      -- design §7 — `localSub = id`). The node's `layer` (dropped by `RootLedger`) advances on the
   216	      -- child; `StepRel`'s eligibility ∨ exempts a rollover edge (not case11/case12), so it stays
   217	      -- rfl-class. Matches `ConState.stepRollover`'s `toRootLedger` exactly.
   218	      { numDiv := n.numDiv
   219	        divExp := n.divExp
   220	        divProfile := n.divProfile

codex

import sympy as sp

# Exceptional coordinates and chart-tail coordinate.
u11, u12, beta, gamma = sp.symbols('u11 u12 beta gamma')
# Current (recoordinatized) layer-2 matrix and layer-3 matrix.
b00,b01,b10,b11 = sp.symbols('b00 b01 b10 b11')
z00,z01,z10,z11 = sp.symbols('z00 z01 z10 z11')
B = sp.Matrix([[b00,b01],[b10,b11]])
Z = sp.Matrix([[z00,z01],[z10,z11]])
center = (u12,b00,b10)                 # pivot + layer-2 column 0
support = (b00,b01,b10,b11)            # full layer-2 block
allvars = (u11,u12,beta,gamma,b00,b01,b10,b11,z00,z01,z10,z11)

def center_degrees(f, vars_):
    p = sp.Poly(sp.expand(f), *vars_)
    return [sum(m) for m in p.monoms()]

def zero_on_center(f):
    return sp.expand(f).subs({v:0 for v in center}, simultaneous=True) == 0

def joint_deg_le_one(f):
    return max(center_degrees(f, center), default=0) <= 1

def exact_center_deg_one(f):
    ds = center_degrees(f, center)
    return bool(ds) and set(ds) == {1}

def boost(f):
    # blockBlowupMap C pivot: pivot fixed, other center coordinates gain pivot.
    return sp.expand(f.subs({b00:u12*b00, b10:u12*b10}, simultaneous=True))

def divisible_by_pivot(f):
    f = sp.expand(f)
    q = sp.cancel(f/u12)
    return sp.expand(f.subs(u12,0)) == 0 and bool(q.is_polynomial(*allvars))

def matrix_checks(name, R):
    fs = list(R)
    a1 = all(zero_on_center(f) for f in fs)
    a2 = all(joint_deg_le_one(f) for f in fs)
    exact = all(exact_center_deg_one(f) for f in fs)
    pulled = [boost(f) for f in fs]
    a3 = all(divisible_by_pivot(f) for f in pulled)
    print(name)
    print('  residual =')
    sp.print_latex(R)
    print('  A1 =', a1, 'A2 =', a2, 'exact-degree-1 =', exact, 'A3 =', a3)
    if not a1:
        for i,f in enumerate(fs):
            rem = sp.expand(f.subs({v:0 for v in center}, simultaneous=True))
            if rem "'!= 0:
                print(f'"'  A1 counterexample entry {i}:', rem)
                break
    if not a3:
        for i,f in enumerate(pulled):
            rem = sp.expand(f.subs(u12,0))
            if rem "'!= 0:
                print(f'"'  A3 counterexample entry {i}, remainder at pivot=0:', rem)
                break
    return a1,a2,a3

# Q row-clear/Schur normal form.  After the first strict transform, the remaining
# 1x1 Schur coordinate is Delta.  Q"'^{-1} contributes the beta upper shear.
Delta = sp.symbols('"'Delta')
Qinv = sp.Matrix([[1,beta],[0,1]])
K_Delta = sp.diag(1,Delta) * Qinv
R_after_first_quotient = sp.expand(Z * B * K_Delta)

# Actual fold: first edge delta=1; second edge starts at J=1, hence delta=0.
# Its singleton blockBlowupMap is the identity/rename Delta -> u12.
R_actual = R_after_first_quotient.subs(Delta,u12)
actual = matrix_checks('ACTUAL fold (delta sequence 1,0; Delta -> u12)', R_actual)

# Literal requested variant: applying a second quotient map sets the second pivot to 1.
R_two_quotients = R_after_first_quotient.subs(Delta,1)
literal = matrix_checks('LITERAL two-quotient variant (Delta -> 1)', R_two_quotients)

# Logical countermodel to derivability from full-support Deg1 + the same ledger/foldB:
# omit the Schur clearing.  This residual is exactly degree one on the full B block,
# and has the same exceptional/ledger variables, but is not ready on the boost center.
K_unprepared = sp.Matrix([[1,beta],[gamma,u12]])
R_unprepared = sp.expand(Z * B * K_unprepared)
full_support_deg1 = all(exact_center_deg_one(f) for f in [])  # unused guard
full_support_deg1 = all(set(center_degrees(f, support)) == {1} for f in list(R_unprepared))
bad = matrix_checks('UNPREPARED admissible algebraic countermodel', R_unprepared)
print('  full-support exact Deg1 =', full_support_deg1)
print('  same dominant scalar foldB can be u11; no formula above constrains it further')

# State/path check from delta=[J=0].
S,J = 1,0
trace=[]
for edge in ('case2','case2','rollover','case11-target'):
    delta = int(J == 0)
    trace.append((S,J,edge,delta))
    if edge == 'case2': J += 1
    elif edge == 'rollover': S,J = S+1,0
print('PATH TRACE (1-indexed S as in the question):')
for row in trace: print(' ', row)

assert actual == (True,True,True)
assert literal == (False,True,False)
assert full_support_deg1 and bad[0] is False
assert trace[0][-1] == 1 and trace[1][-1] == 0 and trace[3][-1] == 1
print('ALL ASSERTIONS PASSED')

codex
## Verdict

The proposed path has one error: since \(\delta=[J=0]\), the second layer-1 edge starts at \(J=1\), so it has \(\delta=0\), not \(\delta=1\).

The actual trace is

\[
(1,0)\xrightarrow[\delta=1]{\mathrm{case2}}(1,1)
\xrightarrow[\delta=0]{\mathrm{case2}}(1,2)
\xrightarrow{\mathrm{rollover}}(2,0)
\xrightarrow[\delta=1]{\mathrm{case11}}\cdots
\]

This agrees with the b-chain \((u_{11},u_{11}u_{12})\): \(u_{12}\) is non-dominant at \(p\), so `foldB p = u₁₁`, not \(u_{11}u_{12}\).

| Interpretation | A1 | A2 | A3 |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Actual fold, δ-sequence \(1,0\) | TRUE | TRUE | TRUE |
| Literal “two quotient maps” | FALSE | TRUE | FALSE |

Let \(s=u_{12}\), let \(B=(b_{ij})\) be the recoordinatized layer-2 matrix, \(Z=C^{(3)}\), and \(H=ZB=(h_{ij})\). The actual residual is

\[
R_p
=H
\begin{pmatrix}1&\beta\\0&s\end{pmatrix}
=
\begin{pmatrix}
h_{00}&\beta h_{00}+s h_{01}\\
h_{10}&\beta h_{10}+s h_{11}
\end{pmatrix}.
\]

For the center \(C=\{s,b_{00},b_{10}\}\), every monomial contains exactly one center variable. Hence A1 and A2 hold. Under the boost \(b_{00},b_{10}\mapsto s b_{00},s b_{10}\), every entry acquires an exact factor \(s\), proving A3.

If the second edge is incorrectly quotiented, \(s\mapsto1\). Then, after setting the center to zero, entry \((0,1)\) leaves

\[
b_{01}z_{00}+b_{11}z_{01}\neq0,
\]

which also obstructs divisibility in A3.

## Architecture verdict

Needs-carrying under the current `IsRealBranch`/`foldB` interface.

More precisely, the needed invariant is weighted b-chain compatibility, not merely boost-readiness at one center. `foldB p` records only the dominant monomial \(u_{11}\); it does not encode the non-dominant ratio \(b_2/b_1=u_{12}\).

There is an exact countermodel with the same full-support Deg1 property and the same scalar `foldB`:

\[
R_{\mathrm{bad}}
=ZB
\begin{pmatrix}1&\beta\\ \gamma&s\end{pmatrix}.
\]

It is exactly degree one in the full \(B\)-block, but center-zeroing leaves

\[
\gamma(b_{01}z_{00}+b_{11}z_{01}),
\]

so it is not boost-ready. This corresponds to omitting the Schur preparation; the current coarse shear predicate admits such an identity/unprepared shear.

Per edge:

- The first case2 \(\delta=1\) step must establish the Schur-prepared weighted form.
- The second case2 step is \(\delta=0\); it only specializes \(\Delta\mapsto u_{12}\) and cannot create missing divisibility.
- Rollover is identity and merely preserves the property.
- The case1(1) step consumes boost-readiness to obtain exact division; it does not derive it from full-support Deg1.

Thus either carry a weighted invariant, or strengthen `IsRealBranch` to pin the exact canonical Q/Schur shear. With that stronger provenance, it could instead become a derived theorem.

A suitable maintenance statement is:

```lean
theorem bChainCompatible_step
    (hbc : BChainCompatible d e p)
    (hcan : CanonicalQSchurStep d p ed) :
    BChainCompatible d e (p.extend ed)
```

Then `realBranch_boostReady_case11` is a corollary of `BChainCompatible`.

| Actual fold, δ-sequence \(1,0\) | TRUE | TRUE | TRUE |
| Literal “two quotient maps” | FALSE | TRUE | FALSE |

Let \(s=u_{12}\), let \(B=(b_{ij})\) be the recoordinatized layer-2 matrix, \(Z=C^{(3)}\), and \(H=ZB=(h_{ij})\). The actual residual is

\[
R_p
=H
\begin{pmatrix}1&\beta\\0&s\end{pmatrix}
=
\begin{pmatrix}
h_{00}&\beta h_{00}+s h_{01}\\
h_{10}&\beta h_{10}+s h_{11}
\end{pmatrix}.
\]

For the center \(C=\{s,b_{00},b_{10}\}\), every monomial contains exactly one center variable. Hence A1 and A2 hold. Under the boost \(b_{00},b_{10}\mapsto s b_{00},s b_{10}\), every entry acquires an exact factor \(s\), proving A3.

If the second edge is incorrectly quotiented, \(s\mapsto1\). Then, after setting the center to zero, entry \((0,1)\) leaves

\[
b_{01}z_{00}+b_{11}z_{01}\neq0,
\]

which also obstructs divisibility in A3.

## Architecture verdict

Needs-carrying under the current `IsRealBranch`/`foldB` interface.

More precisely, the needed invariant is weighted b-chain compatibility, not merely boost-readiness at one center. `foldB p` records only the dominant monomial \(u_{11}\); it does not encode the non-dominant ratio \(b_2/b_1=u_{12}\).

There is an exact countermodel with the same full-support Deg1 property and the same scalar `foldB`:

\[
R_{\mathrm{bad}}
=ZB
\begin{pmatrix}1&\beta\\ \gamma&s\end{pmatrix}.
\]

It is exactly degree one in the full \(B\)-block, but center-zeroing leaves

\[
\gamma(b_{01}z_{00}+b_{11}z_{01}),
\]

so it is not boost-ready. This corresponds to omitting the Schur preparation; the current coarse shear predicate admits such an identity/unprepared shear.

Per edge:

- The first case2 \(\delta=1\) step must establish the Schur-prepared weighted form.
- The second case2 step is \(\delta=0\); it only specializes \(\Delta\mapsto u_{12}\) and cannot create missing divisibility.
- Rollover is identity and merely preserves the property.
- The case1(1) step consumes boost-readiness to obtain exact division; it does not derive it from full-support Deg1.

Thus either carry a weighted invariant, or strengthen `IsRealBranch` to pin the exact canonical Q/Schur shear. With that stronger provenance, it could instead become a derived theorem.

A suitable maintenance statement is:

```lean
theorem bChainCompatible_step
    (hbc : BChainCompatible d e p)
    (hcan : CanonicalQSchurStep d p ed) :
    BChainCompatible d e (p.extend ed)
```

Then `realBranch_boostReady_case11` is a corollary of `BChainCompatible`.

## SymPy script run

```python
import sympy as sp

u11,u12,beta,gamma = sp.symbols('u11 u12 beta gamma')
b00,b01,b10,b11 = sp.symbols('b00 b01 b10 b11')
z00,z01,z10,z11 = sp.symbols('z00 z01 z10 z11')
Delta = sp.symbols('Delta')
B = sp.Matrix([[b00,b01],[b10,b11]])
Z = sp.Matrix([[z00,z01],[z10,z11]])
C = (u12,b00,b10)
S = (b00,b01,b10,b11)
allvars = (u11,u12,beta,gamma,*S,z00,z01,z10,z11)

def degs(f, xs):
    return [sum(m) for m in sp.Poly(sp.expand(f), *xs).monoms()]

def checks(R):
    fs = list(R)
    a1 = all(sp.expand(f).subs(
        {x:0 for x in C}, simultaneous=True) == 0 for f in fs)
    a2 = all(max(degs(f,C), default=0) <= 1 for f in fs)
    pulled = [sp.expand(f.subs(
        {b00:u12*b00,b10:u12*b10}, simultaneous=True)) for f in fs]
    a3 = all(
        sp.expand(f.subs(u12,0)) == 0 and
        bool(sp.cancel(f/u12).is_polynomial(*allvars))
        for f in pulled)
    return a1,a2,a3

Qinv = sp.Matrix([[1,beta],[0,1]])
R1 = sp.expand(Z*B*sp.diag(1,Delta)*Qinv)
R_actual = sp.expand(R1.subs(Delta,u12))
R_literal = sp.expand(R1.subs(Delta,1))
R_bad = sp.expand(Z*B*sp.Matrix([[1,beta],[gamma,u12]]))

print('R_actual =', R_actual)
print('actual A1,A2,A3 =', checks(R_actual))
print('R_literal =', R_literal)
print('literal A1,A2,A3 =', checks(R_literal))
print('literal center-zero witness =',
      sp.expand(R_literal[0,1]).subs(
          {x:0 for x in C}, simultaneous=True))
print('bad full-support degree sets =',
      [set(degs(f,S)) for f in list(R_bad)])
print('bad A1,A2,A3 =', checks(R_bad))
print('bad center-zero witness =',
      sp.expand(R_bad[0,0]).subs(
          {x:0 for x in C}, simultaneous=True))

Sidx,J = 1,0
trace=[]
for edge in ('case2','case2','rollover','case11'):
    trace.append((Sidx,J,edge,int(J == 0)))
    if edge == 'case2':
        J += 1
    elif edge == 'rollover':
        Sidx,J = Sidx+1,0
print('trace =', trace)

assert checks(R_actual) == (True,True,True)
assert checks(R_literal) == (False,True,False)
assert all(set(degs(f,S)) == {1} for f in list(R_bad))
assert checks(R_bad)[0] is False
assert [x[3] for x in trace] == [1,0,0,1]
print('PASS')
```
